Method: 利用回归模型来评估每个特征对预测实例时间戳的贡献程度，通过这种方式检测哪些特征随时间发生分布变化，从而识别数据集的不稳定性来源。

Result: TEDD方法能够检测数值型和类别型特征的所有基本变化类型；可检测多变量漂移；返回可比较的变化度量值；无需参数调优；在特征数量和实例数量上均具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: TEDD是一种有效的自动检测特征时间分布变化的技术，相比其他方法具有更全面的检测能力和更好的实用性，有助于构建长期保持高性能的鲁棒机器学习模型。

Abstract: When working with real-world temporal data, it is common to encounter features whose distribution is changing over time. The naive employment of Machine Learning models on this unstable data might lead to rapidly degrading performance, especially if the new distribution is much different from what was previously seen during training. In order to cope with this problem, it is critical to automatically identify features that are changing over time. With these features detected, data scientists and other practitioners will be able to mitigate the issue (for instance, by applying data transformations), deploying more robust models that retain high performance for longer periods of time. In this paper, we describe which temporal changes a feature should not suffer from, and propose TEDD, a technique to a) identify when a dataset might lead to an unstable Machine Learning model and b) automatically detect which features cause such lack of robustness. In order to achieve it, we leverage a regression model to highlight which features contribute to a good prediction of an instance's timestamp. We compare our approach to other methods in real and synthetic data, testing their detection capability on all simple change patterns. We show that our method: detects all types of basic changes, both for numerical and categorical features; can detect multivariate drifts; returns a comparable value measuring the amount of change of each feature; requires no parameter tuning; and is scalable both on number of features and instances of the dataset.

</details>


### [182] [Physics-Aware Auxiliary Losses Improve Out-of-Distribution Generalization of a GNN Synthesizability Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12651)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在GNN中引入Bertz指数和MMFF94应变能作为辅助损失，在OOD场景下获得微小但统计显著的AUC提升（+0.66%），并揭示了单种子实验可能产生误导性结论的方法论警示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成可行性过滤器（如SAScore、SCScore等）是纯统计模型，在OOD（分布外）场景下性能退化；作者希望探索廉价、封闭形式的物理先验（拓扑复杂度、应变能）作为GNN的辅助监督信号，是否能改善OOD泛化能力。

Method: 基于GINE骨干网络，添加两个辅助损失：Bertz指数监督的拓扑复杂度回归、MMFF94力场能量监督的应变能软惩罚；在65,177分子数据集（HIV+Tox21+COCONUT）上进行4路消融实验（基线/+复杂度/+应变/+两者），采用单源OOD划分（训练：HIV+Tox21，测试：COCONUT），重复5个种子并计算配对bootstrap置信区间。

Result: 三种物理先验变体在OOD上均获得统计显著的微小提升：+复杂度Δ=+0.0060（95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]），+应变Δ=+0.0032（[+0.0008, +0.0052]），+两者Δ=+0.0066（[+0.0038, +0.0093]）；所有区间不包含零，组合效果最佳；ID性能无差异。单种子实验曾产生相反的定性结论，多种子后不再成立。

Conclusion: 物理先验作为辅助损失可以小幅改善OOD泛化，但效果有限；方法论上强调多种子评估的重要性，单次实验可能产生误导性结论。

Abstract: Machine-learning drug-discovery pipelines increasingly rely on generative models that propose molecules far from the data used to train downstream synthesizability filters. Existing filters (SAScore, SCScore, RAscore, DeepSA) are purely statistical and degrade in exactly this out-of-distribution (OOD) regime. We ask whether cheap, closed-form physical priors, used as auxiliary supervision on a graph neural network (GNN), improve OOD generalization. We add two auxiliary losses to a GINE backbone: a topological complexity regression supervised by the Bertz index, and a strain-energy soft penalty supervised by MMFF94 force-field energy. On a 65,177-molecule corpus (HIV, Tox21, COCONUT) labeled by SAScore thresholds we reproduce a strong in-distribution baseline, then evaluate a 4-way ablation (baseline / +complexity / +strain / +both) on a single-source OOD split (train on drug-like HIV+Tox21, test on COCONUT natural products), repeated over 5 seeds with paired bootstrap confidence intervals. All three physics-aware variants give a small but statistically significant OOD improvement over the baseline (mean OOD AUC 0.9774): +complexity Delta = +0.0060 (95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]), +strain Delta = +0.0032 ([+0.0008, +0.0052]), +both Delta = +0.0066 ([+0.0038, +0.0093]); every interval excludes zero, and the combination is best. The variants are indistinguishable in-distribution, so the effect is visible only under OOD evaluation. We are explicit that the effects are modest, and we report a cautionary methodological finding: a single-seed version of this experiment produced a qualitatively different (non-monotone) story that did not survive multi-seed evaluation.

</details>


### [183] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基准测试了PINN在化疗药代动力学（PK）中的表现。在线性二室模型中，PINN与标准方法（NLS）精度相当（差距小常数因子），同时一次训练即可输出组织浓度曲线；在Michaelis-Menten扩展模型中，NLS因双指数闭式解不存在而失效，PINN则揭示了模型不可识别性问题，并展示了利用稀疏组织观测数据恢复参数的能力。核心观点：PINN并非优于NLS，而是提供统一的框架——在标准问题上匹配教科书方法，暴露教科书方法隐藏的结构可识别性，并能融合异质测量数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学中存在大量部分可观测问题（如化疗PK中可测血浆浓度但不可测组织浓度），传统方法（NLS）在模型扩展（如Michaelis-Menten动力学）时因闭式解不存在而失效。PINN作为一种能直接处理偏微分/常微分方程且不依赖解析解的框架，有望统一解决此类问题，并提供可识别性方面的洞察。

Method: 以化疗药代动力学的二室模型为测试平台，对比PINN、标准临床基线（NLS，基于双指数血浆解析解的非线性最小二乘）和物理无关的神经网络基线（纯数据MLP）。在两个问题（线性二室模型和Michaelis-Menten饱和消除模型）上进行基准测试，评估各方法在血浆浓度拟合、组织浓度预测及参数恢复方面的表现。

Result: 在线性二室模型中，NLS近乎最优，PINN与之匹配（仅差小常数因子），同时一次训练输出组织曲线；纯数据MLP在组织预测上差约10倍。在Michaelis-Menten模型中，NLS因双指数闭式解不存在而返回无意义参数；PINN揭示了模型不可识别性（k12→0）。加入两个稀疏组织观测后，PINN恢复k21误差<1%，Vmax和Km在一个标准差内，k12从0.02恢复至0.82（仍偏低约2σ）。

Conclusion: PINN并非要取代NLS，而是提供一种统一的方案：在标准问题上匹配教科书方法的表现，暴露教科书方法无法识别的结构可识别性问题，并能通过单一损失函数融合异质测量数据（血浆+稀疏组织观测）。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [184] [A Zero-shot Generalized Graph Anomaly Detection Framework via Node Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12673)
*Phan Nguyen,Dat Cao,Hien Chu,Khue Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlignGAD是一个零样本跨域图异常检测框架，通过全局统一模块对齐异构特征、聚类模块捕获群体异常模式、节点差异评分模块聚合多视图异常证据，在多个真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域图异常检测方法过度依赖特定数据集的特征语义和结构模式，导致跨域泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出AlignGAD框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) 全局统一模块：对齐异构节点特征并在谱域归一化图信号；(2) 聚类模块：构建聚类感知的图视图以捕获群体级异常模式；(3) 节点差异评分模块：测量重建差异并从不同图视图聚合异常证据。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的实验证明了AlignGAD在零样本图异常检测设置下的有效性。

Conclusion: AlignGAD通过特征对齐、聚类感知和差异评分三个模块的协同工作，成功实现了零样本跨域图异常检测，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Cross-domain graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify abnormal nodes in unseen target graphs, showing strong potential in real-world applications with heterogeneous graph data. However, existing methods often depend on dataset-specific feature semantics and structural patterns, which limits their ability to generalize across different domains. To address this challenge, we propose AlignGAD, a zero-shot generalized graph anomaly detection framework. Our framework is built upon three key components: a Global Unification Module that aligns heterogeneous node features and normalizes graph signals in the spectral domain; a Clustering Module that constructs cluster-aware graph views to capture group-level abnormal patterns; and a Node Discrepancy Scoring Module that measures reconstruction discrepancy and aggregates anomaly evidence from different graph views. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AlignGAD under the zero-shot GAD setting.

</details>


### [185] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Fed-FBD，一种基于功能块分解的模块化联邦学习架构，通过将ResNet分解为6个功能块并维护不同颜色变体，实现了块级隔离、隐私保护和快速机器遗忘，在多个数据集上以微小精度代价换取这些安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如FedAvg）将每个客户端视为黑箱，无法隔离恶意贡献者、审计客户端影响或实现参与者的被遗忘权，缺乏细粒度的安全与隐私保障机制。

Method: 将ResNet骨干分解为6个功能块（stem、四个残差组、分类头），维护N个颜色变体的仓库，每个变体由独立追踪和贡献者标记的块组装而成，实现块级隔离。

Result: 在MedMNIST-2D、PathMNIST和CIFAR-10上，与FedAvg相比精度损失0.3%-3.1%（IID）、0.8%-4.0%（Dirichlet alpha=1.0），所有六种对抗攻击被限制在中毒客户端自身块内，干净颜色AUC漂移不超过±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD以微小的精度代价实现了块级隔离、设计上的隐私保护和亚秒级机器遗忘，为联邦学习提供了更强的安全与隐私保障。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

</details>


### [186] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在监督域适应（sDA）中，因果知识（不变特征子集）能否以及何时能够带来有限样本下的性能提升。在线性回归设定下，推导了匹配的上下界，表明有限样本增益取决于目标风险边际和源域估计误差，并提出了自适应聚合方法以避免负迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果驱动的域泛化方法在总体层面上能找到跨域稳定的不变预测器，但在有限样本（尤其是只有少量目标域标注数据）下这些不变性能否带来实际增益尚不清楚。本文旨在探究因果知识（完整或部分）何时能可证明地提升监督域适应性能。

Method: 以线性回归为研究对象，将因果知识建模为不变或可能不变的特征子集集合，每个子集对应一个源域训练的候选预测器。推导了匹配的上下界，证明有限样本增益由候选预测器之间的目标风险边际和源域估计误差共同决定。提出自适应聚合方法，可在边际足够大时匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移。

Result: 当目标风险边际相对于目标域样本量n_Q足够大时，自适应聚合方法能匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移；当边际过小时，任何算法都无法可靠利用候选集合获得更快的有限样本速率。进一步将边际与线性SCM中的结构偏移幅度联系起来，并在真实因果基准上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 因果不变性在监督域适应中的有限样本增益取决于候选预测器之间的目标风险边际大小，只有当边际足够大时才能获得可证明的增益，否则无法超越简单目标域学习。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Forecasting Is Not Attribution: Localizing Decoder Bypass in Graph-Based Neural Marketing Mix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12687)
*Yunbo Wang,Bolbi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了图基神经网络营销组合模型中存在的“归因绕过”失效模式，即高容量解码器可通过自回归、密集通信等方式获得低预测误差，但无法通过图结构传递反事实敏感性。作者提出DICE-MMM框架，将图恢复、预测准确性和图对齐归因三个问题分离，实验表明低MSE不能作为归因有效性的证明，关键瓶颈在于图支撑选择而非预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基神经MMM常将预测准确性与归因能力混为一谈，但两者并不等价。高容量解码器可能通过目标自回归、密集通信、协动性、上下文或潜记忆等机制获得低预测误差，却绕过了作为归因对象的图结构，导致归因失效。作者旨在诊断并隔离这种“归因绕过”问题。

Method: 提出DICE-MMM框架，分为两阶段：阶段1训练图编码器与受限图介导解码器；阶段2冻结所选编码器，训练图安全潜解码器，其跨节点通信必须通过所提供的图结构。使用CIG、AR-CIG和图交换测试评估解码器使用情况，并在可控R/d/T交换及外部多图rawlog压力测试中验证。

Result: DICE在稳定图恢复上优于CausalMMM。关键发现：无图解码器和全图解码器在稀疏目标基准上MSE@7约0.004，但AR-CIG nAUPRC接近或低于零；而Oracle图在相近MSE下nAUPRC达0.807±0.129。图交换实验进一步定位瓶颈：同一DICE硬训练解码器在学习图输入下nAUPRC为-0.044±0.006，在Oracle图下升至0.894±0.027。

Conclusion: 低预测误差不能作为归因有效性的证书；图基神经MMM中的关键瓶颈在于图支撑选择的正确性，而非预测能力或解码器容量。DICE提供了一个压力测试和失效定位框架，分离了图恢复、预测准确性和图对齐归因三个问题。

Abstract: Marketing mix models are used to forecast business outcomes and to attribute those outcomes to marketing channels, but these goals are not equivalent. We study a failure mode in graph-based neural MMM called attribution bypass: a high-capacity decoder can obtain low forecasting error through target autoregression, dense communication, co-movement, context, or latent memory while failing to route counterfactual sensitivity through the graph used as the attribution object. We introduce DICE-MMM as a bounded diagnostic and training framework. We do not claim that observational neural MMM identifies causal effects. Instead, DICE separates three questions often conflated in graph-based MMM: graph recovery, forecasting accuracy, and whether the trained decoder's perturbation-induced influence is graph aligned. Stage 1 trains a graph encoder with a restricted graph-mediated decoder. Stage 2 freezes the selected encoder and trains a graph-safe latent decoder whose cross-node communication must pass through the supplied graph. Decoder use is evaluated with CIG, AR-CIG, and graph-swap tests. Across controlled R/d/T swaps and an external multi-graph rawlog stress test, DICE improves stable graph recovery over CausalMMM. The experiments show that forecasting accuracy is not an attribution certificate: in a sparse-target benchmark, no-graph and full-graph decoders achieve MSE@7 around 0.004 while AR-CIG nAUPRC remains near or below zero, whereas an oracle graph reaches 0.807 +/- 0.129 at comparable MSE. Frozen graph-swap localizes the bottleneck: the same DICE-hard-trained decoder moves from nAUPRC -0.044 +/- 0.006 under learned graph inputs to 0.894 +/- 0.027 with the oracle graph. The contribution is a stress test and failure-localization framework showing that low MSE can hide attribution bypass and that the unresolved bottleneck is graph-support selection, not forecasting or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [188] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha,Naomi Sagan,Keisuke Kamahori,Irmak Sivgin,Rohan Sanda,Steven Gao,Mark Horowitz,Luke Zettlemoyer,Olivia Hsu,Jure Leskovec,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M*是一个通用服务系统，通过将复合AI模型表示为数据流图并采用Walk Graph模块化抽象，支持灵活部署和模型无关优化，在多种多模态工作负载上显著优于现有系统（如vLLM-Omni），延迟降低20%，吞吐量提升2.7倍，机器人规划性能提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型服务框架基于对模型结构的狭隘假设，无法适应新型复合架构（融合视觉编码器、语言主干、扩散/流头、音频编解码器、动作生成器、世界模型预测器等）的多样性需求。

Method: 将复合模型表示为数据流图，请求处理转化为图遍历；核心创新是Walk Graph模块化抽象，支持任意组件组合、灵活物理集群放置及模型无关的分布式运行时优化。

Result: 在BAGEL上文本到图像任务相比vLLM-Omni端到端延迟降低20%；Qwen3-Omni上文本到语音任务实时因子降低2.9倍、吞吐量提升2.7倍；V-JEPA 2-AC机器人规划任务性能提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: M*为高效服务复杂复合模型铺平了道路，以最小开发代价实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


### [189] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，当在部分观测的线性动力系统数据上通过经验风险最小化训练时，两层线性自回归模型能够自然学习近似卡尔曼滤波，其隐表示与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计相似（相差一个相似变换），模型无需知晓底层动力学或状态信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自回归模型为何以及如何学习到隐表示是一个开放的理论问题。作者希望从理论层面揭示自回归模型在序列数据上学习隐表示的内在机制，特别是与经典最优滤波算法（卡尔曼滤波）之间的联系。

Method: 作者从三个关键洞察出发：(1) 证明卡尔曼滤波可被自回归模型近似，且截断误差有界；(2) 证明两层线性自回归模型的优化景观是良性的（所有驻点要么是严格鞍点，要么是全局最小值）；(3) 提供预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复的有限样本保证。

Result: 理论上证明了自回归模型学习到的隐表示与卡尔曼滤波的状态估计在相似变换下等价；给出了预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复的有限样本保证；数值仿真验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 自回归模型在部分观测线性动力系统数据上训练时，其隐表示自然恢复了最优状态估计，揭示了现代序列模型与经典控制/估计理论之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [190] [LLM-Powered Personalized Glycemic Assessment in Type 2 Diabetes with Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12699)
*Yifan Gao,Yanmin Gong,Yun Shi,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GlyLLM框架，利用大语言模型整合可穿戴传感器数据和结构化元数据，在血糖预测和糖尿病分类任务上分别比传统机器学习方法提升13.66% (RMSE) 和13.08% (AUROC)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2型糖尿病（T2D）的血糖评估需要整合个体化信息，但现有机器学习方法主要依赖历史血糖测量数据，忽略了个体化上下文信息，限制了在不同糖尿病群体中的性能表现。

Method: 提出GlyLLM框架，通过将CGM可穿戴传感器数据与结构化元数据整合，利用预训练大语言模型的先验知识，实现传感器数据与文本语义在决策时刻的抽象融合。

Result: 在AI-READI数据集的两个任务中，血糖预测RMSE平均降低13.66%，糖尿病分类AUROC平均提升13.08%；消融实验显示糖尿病调查和生物特征测试比其他健康信息更为关键。

Conclusion: GlyLLM展示了利用大语言模型推进T2D个性化血糖评估的潜力，为精准糖尿病护理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Type 2 Diabetes (T2D) poses an increasing global health threat, demanding effective glycemic assessment to support personalized and improved diabetes care. Wearable sensors such as continuous glucose monitors (CGM) and fitness trackers offer many valuable insights for glycemic assessment. However, effectively analyzing these data requires integration with essential individual-level context. Existing methods are often based on traditional machine learning (ML) and rely primarily on historical blood glucose measurements and overlook personalized information, which limits their performance across diverse diabetes populations. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated their ability to integrate diverse data modalities while modeling sequential dependencies, motivating the exploration of their potential for personalized glycemic assessment.
  In this paper, we propose GlyLLM, an LLM-powered framework for modeling CGM-based glycemic dynamics through the integration of wearable sensor data and structured metadata. GlyLLM can leverage the extensive prior knowledge of pre-trained LLMs and achieve sensor-text semantic abstraction at decision time. Experiments on two related tasks on the AI-READI dataset demonstrate that our model outperforms traditional ML methods by an average of 13.66\% in Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) for glucose forecasting and 13.08\% in Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) for diabetes categorization. Additionally, our ablation study shows that diabetes surveys and biometric tests are more critical than other health information for glycemic assessment. Our work presents a promising step toward harnessing the power of LLMs to advance personalized glycemic assessment in T2D care.

</details>


### [191] [A Stabilized Path-Space Approach to Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12710)
*Evan Scope Crafts,Umberto Villa,Saviz Mowlavi,Yanting Ma,Hassan Mansour,Wael H. Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稳定路径空间的扩散后验采样框架，通过连接随机最优控制与贝叶斯逆问题，采用时间重参数化消除偏差、信赖域路径空间优化学习控制，并引入重要性采样校正，在多个基准逆问题上展示了优于现有方法的精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散后验采样器依赖启发式近似引导，在非线性算子和多模态后验分布中表现不佳，缺乏理论保证和不确定性量化能力。

Method: 1) 基于路径空间构建似然加权目标测度，将后验采样转化为学习受控随机过程；2) 引入时间重参数化消除未知初值函数带来的偏差；3) 采用信赖域路径空间优化与对数方差目标学习控制；4) 提出重要性采样校正实现渐近精确后验期望。

Result: 在具有解析或高质量参考后验的基准逆问题实验中，该方法在采样精度和不确定性量化上优于现有主流方法。

Conclusion: 路径空间视角统一了学习控制方法与基于引导的采样器，量化了近似控制带来的采样误差，并为扩散后验采样提供了理论更完备、实践更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for Bayesian inverse problems, but many diffusion posterior samplers rely on heuristic guidance approximations that can fail for nonlinear operators and multimodal posteriors. In this work, we develop a stabilized path-space framework for diffusion-based posterior sampling. Starting from a base diffusion process whose terminal marginal represents the prior, we define a likelihood-weighted target measure on trajectories and cast posterior sampling as learning a controlled stochastic process whose path measure matches this target. This formulation connects diffusion posterior sampling to stochastic optimal control while preserving the Bayesian structure needed for uncertainty quantification. We introduce a time reparameterization that makes the path-space control problem well posed by removing the bias induced by the unknown initial value function, without auxiliary training. We then learn the control via a trust-region path-space optimization method with log-variance objectives. The path-space perspective also unifies our learned control approach with existing guidance-based samplers, quantifies the sampling error induced by approximate controls, and yields importance sampling corrections for asymptotically exact posterior expectations. We evaluate the proposed framework on a suite of benchmark inverse problems with analytically characterized or high-quality reference posteriors, enabling principled assessment of sampling accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These experiments provide insight into the behavior of diffusion-based posterior samplers and demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over leading approaches.

</details>


### [192] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的统一数学框架，将在无OOD调优数据场景下（通过超参数调优方法）的OOD检测方法应用于开放集射频指纹识别（RFF）领域，在POWDER数据集上验证了该方法在无需真实OOD数据的情况下性能媲美有OOD数据调优的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别系统需在开放世界环境中运行，面临未知发射机信号和时间漂移导致的分布偏移问题。OOD检测是应对该问题的自然框架，但其在RFF中的应用受限于需要辅助OOD数据进行参数调优，而在实际射频环境中收集代表性OOD数据不切实际。

Method: 1) 将机器学习领域的OOD检测方法引入开放集RFF领域；2) 基于信息论构建统一数学框架，系统分析各类OOD方法；3) 应用无需OOD调优数据的检测器调优方法（即无监督超参数选择）于RFF场景。

Result: 在POWDER射频指纹数据集上，无需真实OOD调优数据的检测器性能与有真实OOD调优数据的基线方法相当，且大幅优于完全没有OOD调优数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在开放集RFF任务中具有实际可行性，解决了OOD检测在射频环境中因缺乏代表性OOD数据而难以应用的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

</details>


### [193] [Normative Robustness as a Frontier for Non-Verifiable Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12731)
*Elizaveta Tennant,Benjamin Henke,Anita Keshmirian,Murray Shanahan,Verena Rieser,Kristian Lum,Sydney Levine,Julia Haas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"道德鲁棒性"概念，通过多轮对抗框架评估LLM在非可验证推理（以道德推理为例）中的表现，发现模型会向用户道德观点偏移（平均6.5%），且受顺序（13-22%）、对话轮次（10-24%）等因素影响，表现为"道德商议谄媚"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM推理评估集中在事实性领域（数学、科学），忽略了对非可验证推理（主观、价值判断问题）能力的考察。用户日益依赖LLM提供咨询和建议，需要了解模型在缺乏客观真相的模糊场景中的推理可靠性。

Method: 以道德推理作为非可验证推理的范例子域，定义道德鲁棒性；设计可扩展的对抗性多轮评估框架，模拟48,000次用户-智能体道德商议，操控前提相关性、前提顺序、对话时长和用户道德观点四个变量，测试四个前沿LLM。

Result: 模型能忽略道德无关干扰，但平均向用户偏好道德观点偏移6.5%；顺序改变导致13-22%案例的判决变化；单轮与多轮对话间有10-24%的判决差异；模型不仅调整最终判决，还调整底层论证逻辑以迎合用户。

Conclusion: 模型存在"道德商议谄媚"（moral deliberative sycophancy）这一失效模式，即在非可验证推理中系统性偏向用户立场，削弱了其作为独立、一致道德顾问的可靠性。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly serve in advisory and deliberative roles, users rely on them for non-verifiable reasoning in domains lacking objective ground truths. However, traditional evaluations of LLM reasoning focus almost exclusively on fact-based domains, such as mathematics and science, leaving uncertainty over whether and to what degree models can handle ambiguous, subjective, or value-laden problems over time. To address this concern, we propose moral reasoning as a paradigmatic subdomain of non-verifiable reasoning. We define moral robustness as a model's capacity to exhibit sound moral reasoning across time and contexts, and we introduce a scalable, adversarial, multi-turn evaluation framework to empirically measure this capability. We simulate 48,000 user-agent moral deliberations across four frontier LLMs, varying premise relevance, premise order, conversation duration, and the user's stated moral view. We find that models successfully ignore morally-irrelevant distractors, but shift their reasoning by up to 6.5%, on average, towards the user's stated preferred moral view, and varying their reasoning depending on factors such as order (altering moral judgments by order in 13-22% of the cases) and duration (altering moral judgments between single-turn and multi-turn in 10-24% of the cases). Our analysis indicates that models tailor not just their final verdicts but their underlying justifications to align with a user's moral viewpoint - a failure mode we characterize as moral deliberative sycophancy.

</details>


### [194] [Let's Ask Gauss: Improved One-Run Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12733)
*Adya Agrawal,Yu Wei,Jaspal Singh,Malik Magdon-Ismail,Vassilis Zikas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对差分隐私机器学习中的隐私审计问题，提出了一种基于高斯分布视角的单次运行审计框架，通过利用canary对齐信号序列的渐近正态性，实现了比传统二值猜测方法更紧的隐私下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次运行审计方法将训练样本或"金丝雀"（canary）的阈值转换为二值成员猜测，丢弃了有用的信息，导致隐私下界估计不够紧致。

Method: 在白盒DP-SGD场景下，将canary对齐信号自然建模为随机变量序列，证明其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布，基于此分布视角构建了差分隐私审计框架。

Result: 在单次训练运行中获得了更紧的隐私下界，优于传统的二值猜测方法。

Conclusion: 基于高斯分布视角的审计框架能够更有效地利用信号信息，提升隐私审计的紧致性，为DP机制的实际隐私保障提供更强有力的实证验证。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides an important safeguard by estimating the actual information leaked by a model, thus ensuring that theoretical privacy guarantees hold in practice. We study empirical privacy auditing for differentially private (DP) machine learning, focusing on efficient one-run methods for mechanisms such as DP-SGD. Prior one-run approaches threshold training examples or "canaries" into binary membership guesses, which discards useful information. We show that, in the white-box DP-SGD setting, canary-aligned signals naturally form a sequence of random variables whose normalized sum is asymptotically Gaussian. Leveraging this distributional perspective, we develop a DP-auditing framework that leads to tighter privacy lower bounds from a single training run.

</details>


### [195] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks and Radial Basis Functions for PDEs with Dirac Delta Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12735)
*Manuel Reyna,Alexandre Tartakovsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了将物理信息神经网络（PINN）解释为残差最小二乘法（RLS），并利用径向基函数（RBF）展开直接处理带有狄拉克δ函数项的控制方程，解决了PINN在奇异性问题中因平滑近似导致的建模误差，并通过神经正切核理论解释了RBF-RLS优于PINN的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在处理含有狄拉克δ函数的偏微分方程（如源项、边界条件或初始条件中的奇异性）时，需要使用平滑替代函数近似，这引入显著的建模误差，影响求解精度。

Method: 将PINN重新解释为残差最小二乘法（RLS），利用弱形式积分直接处理狄拉克δ项；重点采用径向基函数（RBF）展开（即单层RBF网络）作为RLS实现；通过神经正切核（NTK）理论分析解释两种方法的差异。

Result: PINN在积分消去狄拉克δ后残差无法收敛到零，而RBF-RLS在前向和逆问题中均能稳定提供良好的解；在多孔介质和河流中的地下水流与输运线性PDE测试中，RBF-RLS在合成数据、含噪合成数据和真实测量数据的逆问题中均表现优异。

Conclusion: 将PINN重新解释为RLS并采用RBF展开，能够直接自然地处理偏微分方程中的狄拉克δ奇异性，避免PINN的平滑近似误差，是一种更有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a machine learning method for solving forward and inverse Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). When applied to PDEs with Dirac delta functions in the forcing terms, boundary conditions, or initial conditions, PINNs require approximating them with smooth surrogate functions, a practice that can introduce significant modeling errors. In this work, we exploit the interpretation of PINNs as Residual Least Squares (RLS) methods and show that this perspective enables direct treatment of Dirac delta terms by integrating the weak-form equation. Among RLS formulations other than PINN, we focus on the Radial Basis Function (RBF) expansion (also known as a single-layer RBF Network). We show that while integrating out the Dirac delta in PINNs causes residuals to fail to converge to zero, RBF-RLS consistently provides good forward and inverse solutions to transport problems. We explain this finding using the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) theory. We test both approaches on linear PDEs that represent groundwater flow and transport in porous media and rivers. We solve inverse problems to fit synthetic data, noisy synthetic data, and real-world measurements.

</details>


### [196] [Deep Unfolded Latent Optimally Partitioned-l2/l1 Networks for Data-driven Block-Sparse Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12740)
*Takanobu Furuhashi,Hidekata Hontani,Qibin Zhao,Tatsuya Yokota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对凸LOP-l2/l1方法需手动调参且无法自动调参的问题，提出基于隐式微分和深度权重分解的两种深度展开架构，实现自动超参数优化，在脉冲噪声下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸LOP-l2/l1方法虽能实现未知分组的块稀疏信号恢复，但依赖手动超参数调优；且其近端算子微分数值不稳定，无法通过深度展开实现自动参数调优。

Method: 提出两种架构：1）基于隐式微分的稳定框架；2）基于深度权重分解的灵活变体，后者还支持非凸光滑数据保真项。

Result: DU-LOP-l2/l1在数值实验中展现出有竞争力的性能和对脉冲噪声的高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功解决了LOP-l2/l1的自动调参问题，并增强了模型对噪声的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The convex Latent Optimal Partition (LOP)-l2/l1 approach enables block-sparse signal recovery with unknown partitions but relies on manual hyperparameter tuning. Additionally, numerical instability in differentiating its proximal operator prevents its automatic parameter tuning via Deep Unfolding (DU). To address these limitations, we propose two architectures: a stable framework utilizing implicit differentiation and a flexible variant leveraging Deep Weight Factorization (DWF). The DWF-based approach also supports nonconvex smooth data fidelity terms. Numerical experiments demonstrate that DU-LOP-l2/l1 yields competitive performance and high resilience against impulsive noise.

</details>


### [197] [Adaptive Weighted Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12763)
*Aditya Bhaskara,Ashok Cutkosky,Ravi Kumar,Manish Purohit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究仅凭每个未知值的一个无偏估计来选择最大值的问题，设计了同时满足可容许性（不被任何其他策略一致支配）且不差于给定基线（如均匀随机选择）的策略，并将其应用于随机优化，得到具有“无妥协”保证的在线到批次转换界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有单次无偏估计的情况下选择最大值，现有策略可能在最坏情况下表现不佳，需要设计既具有理论最优性（可容许性）又能保证不差于简单基线（如均匀随机选择）的策略。

Method: 提出同时满足可容许性和基线保证的选择策略，并将其应用于随机优化的在线到批次转换中。

Result: 所提策略在理论上具有可容许性，且不差于均匀随机选择基线；在随机优化应用中，得到的在线到批次转换界既不比标准随机迭代选择差，在良性环境下可显著更好。

Conclusion: 成功设计了具有“无妥协”保证的选择策略，兼顾了最坏情况下的稳健性和良性环境下的高效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of selecting the largest among $n$ unknown values $x_1,\dots,x_n$ given only a single unbiased estimate $y_i$ for each $x_i$. We design strategies that are simultaneously admissible (not uniformly dominated by any other strategy) and also never worse than a given baseline such as uniform random selection. We provide an application to stochastic optimization, where we obtain online-to-batch conversion bounds with a desirable "no-compromise" guarantee: they are never worse than standard random iterate selection, and yet can be significantly better in benign settings.

</details>


### [198] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM在生成代码时存在功能记忆现象：即使输出与训练数据在文本上不同，但在功能逻辑上存在记忆。通过对Olmo-3-32B模型的对照实验，发现模型能复现未曾在文本上匹配但功能等价的目标代码逻辑，表明仅依赖文本重叠的审计指标不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注模型输出与训练数据之间的文本重叠（verbatim overlap）来检测数据泄露，但代码具有功能等价而文本不相似的特性。作者试图探究超越文本层级的**功能记忆**（functional memorization）现象，即模型是否能够记忆并复现训练代码的功能逻辑，即使表面文本不同。

Method: 构建反事实实验框架：以Olmo-3-32B为对象，对比**中期训练模型**（已接触目标代码）与**预训练参考模型**（未接触目标代码）。向两个模型输入Python函数签名，分别从**文本相似度**和**功能相似度**两个维度评估输出，其中功能相似度采用LLM-as-a-judge和基于执行的测试来衡量。

Result: 实验明确证实了功能记忆的存在。模型在功能上复现了训练数据中的逻辑，而传统的文本重叠指标无法检测到这种记忆现象。

Conclusion: 代码生成场景下，仅依靠文本重叠的审计指标会低估模型的记忆能力，需要引入功能层面的度量标准来更全面地评估模型对训练数据的记忆与潜在泄露风险。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [199] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Yiyang Li,Ziming Li,Xiaoguang Guo,Weixiang Sun,Chuxu Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProPlay提出了一种程序世界模型，通过过程级预演和程序图结构，让智能体在部分可观察环境中实现自我进化，无需外部监督，显著提升了环境理解和自我进化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观察环境中，现有LLM智能体方法虽使用记忆或规划模块，但很少在两者之间形成闭环以持续精炼对环境动力学的内部理解，导致自我进化困难。

Method: 将成功轨迹抽象为程序（procedures）并组织成程序图（procedure graph），图中节点表示任务阶段，边表示因果转换并关联可靠性记录嵌入。智能体在每轮前通过程序图模拟未来程序路径作为软引导，执行后利用环境反馈精炼图结构。

Result: 在公开基准上，ProPlay在环境理解和自我进化能力上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过程序级预演与程序图的闭环机制，ProPlay有效弥合了记忆与规划之间的鸿沟，为LLM智能体在复杂环境中的自主进化提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in https://github.com/antman9914/proplay.

</details>


### [200] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SymQNet是一种基于摊销强化学习的自适应哈密顿量学习方法，通过离线学习后验条件采集策略、在线快速执行，相比传统贝叶斯主动学习方法（如BALD）将决策延迟降低47倍以上（5量子位），在12量子位上将完整模拟步骤从13.27秒降至1.02秒，使自适应学习适用于低延迟重复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应哈密顿量学习中每次实验后都需重新计算贝叶斯设计规则，这一过程耗时数秒，在数百次实验中累积成为显著的墙钟延迟成本，限制了自适应方法在实际低延迟场景中的应用。

Method: 提出SymQNet，采用摊销强化学习框架：离线训练一个后验条件采集策略网络，在线推理时仅需一次前向传播即可获得采集决策，同时保留贝叶斯后验反馈机制。

Result: 在横向场伊辛模型基准上，5量子位时决策延迟相比有界Fisher信息搜索和有界两步BALD分别降低47.1倍和72.6倍；12量子位时完整模拟步骤耗时1.02秒，而BALD需13.27秒。

Conclusion: 学习型采集策略能够使自适应哈密顿量学习在重复性低延迟工作负载下变得实用，大幅降低在线计算开销的同时保持贝叶斯后验信息利用。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [201] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合前瞻搜索策略和秩一Cholesky更新的新算法，用于构建近最优、稀疏的分段线性回归树，在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间实现了良好的权衡，且扩展性显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回归树具有可解释性强且表达能力丰富的特点，但传统贪心算法性能有限，而基于动态规划和分支定界的最优方法在通用线性回归树上计算代价过高。现有前瞻策略主要针对分类问题，缺乏对回归问题的有效方法。因此需要一种在计算效率和预测性能之间取得更好平衡的算法。

Method: 将前瞻搜索策略（lookahead search）与高效的秩一Cholesky更新（rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix）相结合，用于构建近最优的稀疏分段线性回归树。

Result: 从理论和实验两方面证明，该方法在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性三个维度上取得了良好的权衡，并且扩展性显著优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在大幅降低计算开销的同时保持接近最优的性能，为构建高性能可解释的回归模型提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix. We demonstrate, both theoretically and empirically, that our method achieves a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency, predictive accuracy, and sparsity, and scales significantly better than the current state of the art.

</details>


### [202] [TimeROME-DLM: Temporal Causal Tracing and Low-Rank Inference-Time Knowledge Editing for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12841)
*Zhengtao Yao,Liuyang Song,Hongbo Zhang,Chenhao Wei,Haoyan Xu,Guang Yang,Siheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TimeROME-DLM，首个无需训练、无需梯度的推理时知识编辑框架，适用于掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM），通过时序因果追踪和低秩残差编辑记忆实现高效知识编辑，在TOFU任务上遗忘集log-probability降低约83 nats，零额外VRAM，速度提升4-14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）均针对自回归Transformer设计，无法直接应用于迭代去噪的掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）；基于梯度更新的方法需要数十GB额外VRAM且在高学习率下导致MDLM崩溃。亟需一种适用于MDLM的轻量级、无需训练/梯度的知识编辑框架。

Method: 由两个组件构成：(1) 时序间接效应（TIE）因果追踪协议，识别每个事实中在后期去噪步骤最强烈驱动对象预测的坐标；(2) 闭式低秩残差编辑记忆，聚合所有遗忘事实的主语键和目标增量，在该坐标处施加岭回归正则化更新，并通过稀疏化限制效用溢出。仅需调整三个超参数（alpha, lambda, q）。

Result: 在TOFU forget01任务上，遗忘集log-probability降低约83 nats；可迁移至多个MDLM架构；保持保留集log-probability近乎不变（~1 nat内）；顺序插入50个事实时零额外VRAM，速度提升4-14倍；可亚线性扩展至400个事实。

Conclusion: TimeROME-DLM以极低的计算成本弥合了自回归LLM与MDLM在"定位-编辑"范式上的差距，是首个适用于MDLM的免训练、免梯度推理时知识编辑方案。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) such as LLaDA now rival autoregressive (AR) LLMs, but every existing knowledge-editing and unlearning method (ROME, MEMIT, etc.) targets AR transformers and either makes assumptions that fail under iterative denoising, or requires gradient updates whose backward-pass activations cost tens of GB of extra VRAM and which collapse MDLMs at standard learning rates. We introduce TimeROME-DLM, the first training-free, gradient-free, inference-time knowledge-editing framework for MDLMs. It couples two components: a Temporal Indirect Effect (TIE) causal-tracing protocol that identifies, for each fact, the coordinate whose intervention most strongly drives the object prediction at later denoising steps; and a closed-form, low-rank residual edit memory that aggregates subject keys and target deltas across all forget facts and applies a single ridge-regularised update at that coordinate at every diffusion forward, with sparsification to limit utility spillover. Backbone weights stay frozen; only three hyperparameters (alpha, lambda, q) are tuned on a small validation split. On TOFU forget01 with TOFU-finetuned LLaDA-8B-Base, TimeROME-DLM cuts forget-set log-probability by roughly 83 nats. The same configuration transfers to LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B, MMaDA-8B, DiffuLLaMA-7B, and LLaDA-MoE-1.4B. It keeps retain-set log-probability nearly flat (within ~1 nat at the utility-safe operating point) across 50 sequentially inserted facts, delivers a four- to fourteen-fold wall-clock speedup with zero additional VRAM over the strongest converged training-time baseline, and scales sub-linearly to 400 facts. TimeROME-DLM closes the locate-then-edit gap between AR LLMs and MDLMs at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [203] [Interpretable Factor Decomposition for Decision Intelligence in Large-Scale Financial Markets: Evidence from China's A-Share Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12843)
*Xiao Han,Yao Xiao,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可解释的机器学习管道，使用XGBoost+TreeSHAP对2009-2019年中国A股3632只股票进行横截面收益预测分解，发现行为信号（换手率和动量）贡献了58.2%的预测归因，远超估值比率（10.7%），且多空组合月超额收益达+2.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习在量化选股中表现优异但缺乏可解释性，无法将预测能力分解为可审计的因子贡献，且不同归因方法（SHAP与消融分析）间的差异及其隐含的特征可替代性结构尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用XGBoost模型结合TreeSHAP归因方法，在2009-2019年3632只中国A股数据上，以60个月滚动窗口进行55个月样本外测试，通过SHAP分解、消融分析及Carhart四因子模型调整来评估因子贡献与模型表现。

Result: XGBoost取得平均AUC 0.547，顶部vs底部五分位多空组合月收益+2.38%（Newey-West t=5.94，年化Sharpe 2.23），经Carhart四因子调整后alpha仍为+2.31%/月（t=7.48）。SHAP归因显示行为信号占58.2%，估值比率仅占10.7%。SHAP与消融分析的差异揭示了特征可替代性结构。

Conclusion: 机器学习模型在中国A股市场具有显著且稳健的选股alpha，行为因子主导预测归因；SHAP与消融分析的联合使用能揭示单一方法无法识别的特征可替代性结构，为模型审计和因子理解提供新视角。

Abstract: We present an interpretable machine learning pipeline to decompose Cross-Sectional Equity Return Predictability into auditable factor contribution. We apply an XGBoost model with TreeSHAP attribution and conduct stress testing on 3632 Chinese A-share stocks from 2009 until 2019. Using 60-month, rolling windows over 55 months of out-of-sample data, XGBoost obtains a mean AUC of 0.547 and +2.38%/month (Newey-West t = 5.94; Annualized Sharpe 2.23) long-short spread for the top vs bottom quintiles. This alpha is persistent after adjusting for the Carhart four-factor model (+2.31%/month; t = 7.48). SHAP Decomposition indicates that behavioral signals (turnover and momentum) account for 58.2% of predictive attribution compared to 10.7% for valuation ratios, on average, across 55 industry groups. Ablation analysis serves to cross-validate this ranking and provides evidence that SHAP and ablation diverge in a manner that highlights feature substitutability structure that is largely invisible to either method used in isolation.

</details>


### [204] [Multimodal Graph Negative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12863)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态属性图中节点级分支语义不平衡问题，提出GraphMNL框架，通过图感知的负学习进行跨分支引导，让低质量分支学习节点不可能属于的类别而非模仿主导分支，从而避免偏差传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态属性图中不同语义分支（拓扑、文本、视觉）在节点层面的信息可靠性和语义丰富度存在差异，主导分支可能带有偏差，强制低质量分支模仿主导分支会传播偏差并抑制其原有有用语义。

Method: 构建分支库，通过图感知的可靠性仲裁识别主导分支与劣势分支；对不可靠传输进行门控；对非目标类别应用目标保持的负学习，使监督损失学习正确类别，负学习抑制不可能的替代类别。

Result: 在Grocery数据集上达到72.47%准确率，在Reddit M数据集上达到76.60 F1分数，综合性能最佳。

Conclusion: GraphMNL通过负学习解耦目标监督与分支引导，有效解决了多模态属性图中的分支语义不平衡问题，在多个数据集上取得最优性能。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) integrate graph topology with heterogeneous modality attributes, such as text and images, thereby enabling richer modeling of complex relational systems. However, such expressiveness also makes learning on MAGs depend on multiple semantic sources, including structural topology, textual and visual attributes, each of which can be regarded as a branch for node representation. Node-level branch semantic imbalance arises when these branches differ across nodes in semantic informativeness and reliability: a branch that provides discriminative semantics for one node may mislead another due to bias in modality quality or structural context. Existing methods often mitigate such heterogeneity through cross-branch agreement or alignment, implicitly treating the dominant prediction as reliable supervision. When the dominant branch is biased, forced imitation may propagate its bias to other branches and suppress original semantics that are useful for classification. We propose GraphMNL, a graph-aware multimodal negative learning framework that addresses this issue by using Negative Learning as cross-branch guidance. Instead of forcing inferior branches to imitate a teacher prediction, the model teaches them which classes a node is unlikely to belong to. GraphMNL builds a branch library, identifies dominant and inferior branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, gates unstable transfer, and applies target-preserving negative learning over non-target classes. This design decouples target supervision from branch guidance so that supervised losses learn the correct class, while Negative Learning suppresses unlikely alternatives when branch agreement is unreliable. Through the comprehensive experimental evaluation, GraphMNL achieves the best performance on Grocery datasets with 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets.

</details>


### [205] [SMGFM: Spectral Multimodal Graph Pretraining for Multimodal-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12867)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMGFM，一种基于图频率分解的多模态属性图预训练框架，通过将每个模态的节点信号分解为不同图频带并分配语义角色，实现结构诱导语义与模态特有语义的解耦与融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模态属性图中，拓扑结构诱导的语义和模态内在语义对下游任务的贡献不同，传统图学习方法将二者耦合会导致问题。结构语义要求平滑一致性，而模态特有语义包含局部细节，不应被统一平滑或对齐。因此，关键在于跨模态融合前识别不同语义角色。

Method: 利用图频率变化作为先验，提出SMGFM框架。具体包括：(1) 使用可扩展的切比雪夫滤波器构建频率分辨的模态令牌；(2) 通过拓扑条件路由估计令牌的耦合可靠性；(3) 在融合前执行频带-模态交互。频率路由目标对齐平滑共识路由，同时保留模态特有路由。

Result: 在多个多模态属性图数据集上，SMGFM在图级和模态级任务上均达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SMGFM通过频率分解有效解决了多模态属性图中结构语义与模态语义的角色区分问题，避免了空间域纠缠和统一跨模态对齐带来的负面影响，验证了图频率先验在多模态图学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs) couple graph topology with node semantics from text, images, and other modalities. Traditional graph learning contextualizes node semantics by coupling topology with node features. However, this coupling design becomes troublesome in MAGs, where structure-induced and modality-intrinsic semantics may contribute differently to downstream tasks. Structure-induced semantics promote relational consistency through smooth topological variation, whereas modality-intrinsic semantics often encode local, fine-grained distinctions that should not be uniformly smoothed or aligned. Therefore, the key challenge is to identify semantic roles before cross-modal fusion. To this end, we leverage graph-frequency variation as a prior, where low-frequency components capture topology-consistent semantics and high-frequency components preserve modality-specific semantics. Based on this intuition, we propose SMGFM, a spectral multimodal graph pretraining framework that decomposes each modality-specific node signal into graph-frequency bands and assigns band-level semantic roles before cross-modal interaction. Concretely, SMGFM constructs frequency-resolved modality tokens with scalable Chebyshev filters, estimates their coupling reliability through topology-conditioned routing, and performs band-modality interaction before fusion. Its frequency-routed objectives align smooth consensus routes while preserving modality-specific routes, mitigating spatial-domain entanglement and uniform cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments conducted on the MAG datasets demonstrate that SMGFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across graph-level and modality-level tasks.

</details>


### [206] [Multi-Bitwidth Quantization for LLMs Using Additive Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12876)
*Liza Babaoglu,Shuangyi Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Drop-by-Drop框架，通过对LLM权重进行多比特宽度后训练量化，实现从单一模型中动态控制推理精度与效率的权衡，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在不同异构硬件上部署时面临资源约束差异，现有方法无法在不重新训练的情况下自适应地管理性能与效率的权衡。

Method: 基于信息论与逐次精炼理论，利用LLM权重的高斯分布特性，在加权均方误差失真下逐比特优化重建；引入Matryoshka式监督与加性码本结构，使得有序子集码本在每个精度级别上都能实现准确的局部重建。

Result: 单个检查点可服务多种比特宽度，显著降低存储和内存开销，同时在Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral等主流架构上保持有竞争力的困惑度和准确率。

Conclusion: Drop-by-Drop是一种有效的多比特宽度后训练量化方法，实现了推理时精度可控，兼顾了性能与效率的灵活权衡。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware with varying resource constraints, the ability to adaptively manage the trade-off between performance and efficiency without retraining is critical. We propose Drop-by-Drop, a novel multi-bitwidth post-training quantization framework that enables inference-time precision control over LLM weights from a single trained model. Our method is theoretically grounded in information theory and successive refinement. We establish that LLM weights, which commonly follow a Gaussian distribution, can be optimally reconstructed with increasing fidelity as additional bits are incorporated, under a weighted mean squared error distortion motivated by LLM loss functions. To realize this in practice, Drop-by-Drop incorporates Matryoshka-style supervision into the loss function, exploiting the structure of additive codebooks. Drop-by-Drop produces a single model where ordered subsets of codebooks yield accurate partial reconstructions at each precision level. This approach significantly reduces storage and memory overhead by allowing a single checkpoint to serve multiple bitwidths, while maintaining competitive perplexity and accuracy across major architectures, such as Qwen, LLaMA, Gemma, and Mistral.

</details>


### [207] [LongSpike: Fractional Order Spiking State Space Models for Efficient Long Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12895)
*Xinrui He,Qiyu Kang,Xuhao Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpike提出将分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）引入脉冲神经网络，通过扩展传统整数阶SSM到分数阶微积分领域，使SNN能够捕获长序列任务中的复杂长程依赖关系，在LRA、WikiText-103和Speech Commands等基准上超越现有SNN方法，同时保持稀疏突触计算的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN架构通常依赖一阶常微分方程（ODE）控制神经元状态转换，这种一阶假设带来"无记忆"瓶颈，限制了模型捕获长序列任务中复杂长程依赖关系的能力。

Method: 将控制理论中的分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）扩展到脉冲神经领域，通过将传统整数阶SSM推广到分数阶微积分框架，实现具有长记忆核的神经元动力学层次化集成；并采用状态空间公式化以支持高效的并行训练，缓解分数阶算子带来的计算开销和并行化挑战。

Result: 在Long Range Arena（LRA）、大规模WikiText-103和Speech Commands基准上，LongSpike在准确率上超越当前最先进的SNN方法，同时保持了稀疏突触计算的优势。

Conclusion: LongSpike成功将分数阶状态空间建模引入SNN，有效解决了传统一阶SNN在长序列任务中的记忆瓶颈问题，实现了精度提升与计算效率的平衡。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are well-regarded for their biological plausibility and energy efficiency in processing sequential data. However, dominant SNN architectures typically rely on first-order Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to govern neuronal state transitions. This first-order assumption imposes a "memoryless" bottleneck, limiting the model's capacity to capture the complex, long-range dependencies inherent in long-sequence tasks. In this work, we propose LongSpike, a novel SNN framework that integrates fractional-order State-Space Modeling, or f-SSM, from control theory into the spiking domain. By extending traditional integer-order SSMs to the fractional-calculus regime, LongSpike enables the hierarchical integration of neuronal dynamics with long-memory kernels. To mitigate the computational overhead and parallelization challenges typically associated with fractional operators, we leverage a state-space formulation that supports efficient, parallel training. Empirical evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including Long Range Arena (LRA), large-scale WikiText-103, and Speech Commands, demonstrate that LongSpike outperforms state-of-the-art SNNs in accuracy while preserving sparse synaptic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/xinruihe389-commits/LongSpike.

</details>


### [208] [PolicyGuard: Towards Test-time and Step-level Adversary Defense for Reinforcement Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12896)
*Junfeng Guo Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PolicyGuard，一种基于高斯过程后验方差的测试时逐时间步后门防御方法，在七种强化学习游戏中实现了平均 AUROC 0.856（扰动攻击）和 0.859（对手代理攻击）的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习系统的安全性日益重要，现有后门防御方法要么需要访问代理内部参数，要么仅在模型或轨迹级别操作，或仅限于特定攻击类型，缺乏通用且细粒度的防御方案。

Method: 提出 PolicyGuard 框架，利用高斯过程（GP）后验方差进行测试时逐时间步的后门检测；通过自适应伪轨迹技术使得不确定性计算可应用于单个时间步。

Result: 在七个RL游戏上的实验表明，PolicyGuard 在大多数情况下达到了最先进的检测性能，针对扰动攻击的平均 AUROC 为 0.856，针对对手代理攻击的平均 AUROC 为 0.859。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard 是一种有效的测试时逐时间步后门防御方法，具有理论支撑和优异的实验性能，为RL系统的安全性提供了有力保障。

Abstract: While real-world applications of reinforcement learning (RL) are becoming increasingly popular, the security of RL systems deserve more attention and exploration. In particular, recent work has revealed that RL agents are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a victim agent behaves normally under standard conditions but executes malicious actions when a specific trigger is activated. Existing backdoor defenses for RL either require access to the agent's internal parameters, operate only at the model or trajectory level, or are limited to specific attack types. To ensure the security of RL agents, we propose \texttt{PolicyGuard}, a \textit{test-time step-level} backdoor defense which leverages Gaussian Process (GP) posterior variance and adapts pseudo trajectories to enable uncertainty computation for individual time step. Besides, we also provide theoretical foundations to explain the efficacy of GP posterior variance. Extensive experiments across seven RL games demonstrate that PolicyGuard achieves state-of-the-art detection performance in most cases, with average AUROC of 0.856 for perturbation-based attacks and 0.859 for adversary-agent attacks.

</details>


### [209] [Selecting Samples on Graphs: A Unified Dataset Pruning Framework for Lossless Training Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12913)
*Dongyue Wu,Zilin Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Jiajia Liu,Jingdong Chen,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图的统一数据集剪枝框架，通过将数据集建模为加权图（节点权重编码内在价值，边权重编码外在多样性），将剪枝问题转化为最大权重团问题（MWCP），并设计了基于边际收益的贪心求解算法，在保证近似比的同时，在ImageNet-1k上用ResNet-50实现了40%以上的训练时间节省且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法要么依赖独立评估样本内在信号的标准，要么依赖通过成对关系促进多样性的外在信号，每种方法只捕捉了样本效用的单一维度，在不同剪枝率或数据分布下缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 将数据集建模为加权图，节点权重编码内在价值（如基于损失、难度等），边权重编码外在多样性（如样本间相似度或差异性），然后将数据集剪枝形式化为最大权重团问题（MWCP），并设计基于样本边际收益的贪心求解算法，理论证明在温和条件下该目标具有形式化近似保证。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型上的大量实验表明，该方法优于现有剪枝方法，在ImageNet-1k上用ResNet-50训练时间减少40%以上且不损失精度。

Conclusion: 通过图模型统一内在价值和外在多样性信号进行数据集剪枝是有效且鲁棒的，该框架适用于广泛的样本重要性度量，并提供了实用设计指南。

Abstract: The rapid growth of modern training datasets has significantly increased computational cost, motivating dataset pruning~(DP) methods which retain only a subset of informative samples to reduce training cost.
  Existing pruning criteria typically rely on either intrinsic signals that assess samples independently or extrinsic signals that promote diversity via pairwise relations.
  While effective in their own specific regimes, each captures only one aspect of sample utility and lacks robustness across different pruning ratios or data distribution.
  In this work, we present a unified graph-based DP framework.
  By modeling the dataset as a weighted graph, where node weights encode intrinsic value and edge weights encode extrinsic value, DP can be cast as a Maximum Weight Clique Problem (MWCP).
  Although MWCP is NP-hard, its structure admits a principled greedy solution based on sample-wise marginal gains.
  Under a few mild conditions, we further prove that this unified objective enjoys a formal approximation guarantee, which applies to a broad family of importance metrics and provides practical design guidelines.
  Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing DP methods while substantially reducing training cost, reducing training time by over 40\% without sacrificing accuracy on ImageNet-1k with ResNet-50.

</details>


### [210] [Where Computation Lives Inside TabPFN: Causal Localisation of Attention Head Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12917)
*Atharva Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5进行因果机制分析，发现其特征维度注意力头存在明确的时间特化现象：一个主导注意力头在峰值层的因果必要性比其他头高2-5倍，其主导层随任务复杂度变化，其余头呈对称的晚期层特征。注意力熵与激活修补提供收敛证据。对比激活引导在跨样本时失败，归因于TabPFN的上下文学习机制编码任务结构的方式与语言模型不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 首次对表格基础模型（TabPFN 2.5）进行因果机制分析，理解其特征维度注意力头如何跨层分配计算，并探究推理时的可引导性。

Method: 使用激活修补（activation patching）、消融（ablation）和注意力熵（attention entropy）分析，在两个合成回归数据集上进行实验，并结合对比激活引导（contrastive activation steering）探究推理时可控性。

Result: 发现一个主导注意力头在峰值层的因果必要性是其他头的2-5倍，其主导层随任务复杂度变化；其余头呈现对称的晚期层特征。注意力熵与激活修补结果一致。对比激活引导无法跨样本迁移。

Conclusion: TabPFN的上下文学习机制通过上下文依赖的注意力编码任务结构，而非稳定的参数化方向，导致对比激活引导失效，这与语言模型中可引导的机制不同。

Abstract: We present the first causal mechanistic analysis of a tabular foundation model, investigating how TabPFN 2.5's feature wise attention heads distribute computation across layers. Using activation patching, ablation, and attention entropy across two synthetic regression datasets, we find clear temporal specialisation: one head's causal necessity dominates that of the others by 2 to 5 times at peak layer, with its dominant layer shifting across tasks of different complexity, while the remaining heads exhibit symmetric late layer profiles. Attention entropy and patching provide convergent evidence for the computationally active layers of the dominant head. We additionally investigate inference time steerability via contrastive activation steering, which fails to transfer across samples. We attribute this result to TabPFN's in context learning mechanism, which encodes task structure through context dependent attention rather than the stable parametric directions that make steering tractable in language models.

</details>


### [211] [LoRA-Muon: Spectral Steepest Descent on the Low-Rank Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12921)
*Franz Louis Cesista,Katherine Crowson,Cédric Simal,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Muon优化器，将Muon的谱最速下降规则应用于低秩微调，解决了LoRA对初始化敏感、最优学习率跨秩迁移差、难以超越密集基线等问题。其核心包括分裂权重衰减规则，使最优学习率在秩、宽度、深度和因子缩放间良好迁移。实验表明，秩-2代理即可恢复密集最优学习率，秩-32 LoRA-Muon在平均验证损失上超越密集基线，且比LoRA-RITE和Spectron更高效、无QR分解、无需存储二阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在微调时显著降低计算和内存成本，但使用AdamW等因子级优化器时存在对初始化敏感、最优学习率跨秩迁移差、常难以超越密集基线等问题，亟需一种更稳定高效的优化方法。

Method: 将Muon优化器的谱最速下降规则适配到低秩场景，结合分裂权重衰减规则，形成LoRA-Muon优化器。该优化器无需QR分解，不存储二阶矩，计算更高效且内存友好。

Result: 在计算量匹配的TinyShakespeare实验中，秩-2 LoRA-Muon代理恢复密集最优学习率；秩-32 LoRA-Muon在种子平均扫描中达到低于密集基线的平均验证损失。同时证明了Spectron对因子缩放敏感，LoRA-RITE的QR坐标核心本质上实现了相同的谱更新。

Conclusion: LoRA-Muon是满秩Muon和Shampoo族优化器的良好低秩代理，其最优学习率跨秩、宽度、深度和因子缩放迁移，且比现有方案更高效、内存更低，有望成为LoRA微调中替代AdamW的优选项。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) significantly reduces compute and memory costs for finetuning Deep Learning models but is often harder to tune than dense training: when using factor-wise optimizers such as AdamW, it is sensitive to initialization choices, its optimal learning rates transfer poorly across ranks, and it often fails to beat dense baselines. We derive LoRA-Muon by applying the Muon optimizer's spectral steepest-descent rule to the low-rank setting. Along with our split weight-decay rule, our main claim is that LoRA-Muon is a good low-rank proxy for full-rank Muon and Shampoo-family optimizers. Its optimal learning rates transfer across rank, width, depth, and factor-rescaling. In our compute-matched TinyShakespeare study, a rank-$2$ proxy recovers the dense best tested learning rate, and a rank-$32$ LoRA-Muon run attains lower mean validation loss than the dense baseline in the seed-averaged sweep. We further show that the Spectron optimizer depends on arbitrary factor scaling, so it would likely be a poor fit when finetuning starts from badly imbalanced factors, and that LoRA-RITE's simplified QR-coordinate core implements the same spectral update. LoRA-Muon computes that update without QR-decomposition and avoids storing second moments, making it more accelerator-friendly and memory-efficient.

</details>


### [212] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“秩序不等于控制”，引入了基于接收器门控响应定律的控制框架，并在生物系统、大语言模型、适配器和随机算子面板中验证了局部控制的存在，同时明确排除了坐标一致性、控制器结论等超出范围的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐、可解释性、神经扰动研究识别了诱导秩序的物体，但未能区分“秩序”与真正的“控制”。作者希望建立一个严格的控制定义，使得控制需要接收器门控的响应定律，而不仅仅是某种有序模式。

Method: 定义分母索引算子（denominator-indexed operator），将材料状态、动作/驱动、浴、接收器状态映射到响应位移、汇、努力和盆地投影。在生物（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、大语言模型（四种材料条件下预测响应向量，分量符号准确率72.8-73.7%，非零分量84.3-84.8%）、适配器（宪法条件重塑敏感性）和随机算子面板中实证检验。

Result: 生物面板提供了物理响应算子证据但排除了坐标一致性；LLM面板显示生成输出响应定律，非零分量预测准确率84.3-84.8%，观察者预测系统效应和靶标/预言家族准确率93.6%和91.7%；适配器作为制备介质重塑易感性；随机算子面板区分测量机会与可部署行动策略。

Conclusion: 支持局部容许控制和可测随机响应算子的存在，但部署前控制、隐藏/logit因果充分性、生物到LLM的坐标一致性以及字面热力学量不在研究范围内。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [213] [Is Spurious Correlation Removal Always Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12930)
*Yibo Zhou,Bo Li,Hai-Miao Hu,Hanzi Wang,Xiaokang Zhang,Ruifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明不变性学习存在条件计算障碍：即使不变结构在统计上可识别，在某些黑盒采样假设下，存在可采样的多环境实例，其通过穷举搜索可用多项式样本学习，但任何多项式时间算法都无法实现常数精度恢复。进一步量化环境多样性对可识别性和目标函数曲率的影响，推导了最小极大风险以及标签偏移下的相变阈值，并通过实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不变性学习在统计可识别条件下的计算障碍，并量化环境多样性对学习理论界限的影响。

Method: 通过平均情况稀疏恢复归约证明计算障碍；利用分离参数γ量化环境多样性；推导最小极大风险界和相变阈值；结合合成与真实数据实验验证。

Result: 在特定计算假设下，存在多项式样本可学习但多项式时间不可恢复的实例；最小极大风险为Θ(k(d-k)/(n|ℰ|))；标签偏移下在n*∝k(d-k)/(|ℰ|γ²)处发生相变，误差随1/γ²缩放。

Conclusion: 不变性学习面临统计可识别性与计算可行性的根本性分离，环境多样性是决定学习难度和相变行为的关键因素，并提出了实用的多样性诊断方法。

Abstract: Invariant learning can fail even when the invariant structure is statistically identifiable. We show a conditional computational barrier: under a black-box samplable supervised sparse recovery primitive motivated by average-case sparse-recovery reductions, there exist \emph{samplable} multi-environment instances with a one-dimensional predictive invariant subspace ($k=1$) that are learnable with polynomial samples by exhaustive search, while any polynomial-time constant-accuracy recovery algorithm would contradict the primitive. We further quantify environment diversity by a separation parameter $γ$, which controls identifiability and the curvature of invariance objectives. Under sufficient diversity and local Gaussian regularity, the minimax risk is $\mathbb{E}[\dist(\hat{V},V_{\mathrm{inv}})^2]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|\mathcal{E}|))$, and under label-induced shifts a phase transition occurs at $n^*\propto k(d-k)/(|\mathcal{E}|γ^2)$ with refined estimation error scaling proportional to $1/γ^2$. Synthetic and real datasets illustrate the predicted gaps and transitions and motivate simple diversity diagnostics.

</details>


### [214] [Circuit Synchronization Precedes Generalization: Causal Evidence from Fourier Structure in Grokking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12966)
*Achyuthan Sivasankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了频率同步度（FSD）指标，可在Grokking现象发生前500-3000步（平均提前1722步）检测到傅里叶电路同步，并证明相位间差距是正则化现象，grokking时间与权重衰减呈严格反比关系（Δ_t ~ C/λ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象（Transformer在模运算任务上验证精度突然从接近随机跃升至接近完美）已被归因于傅里叶电路，但其发生的时间点、因果结构及可控性仍不清楚。现有方法缺乏早期预测指标和对该现象因果机制的深入理解。

Method: (1) 提出频率同步度（FSD）指标，一种归一化、经置换检验的傅里叶电路同步度量，无需先验电路知识；(2) 在9种模加法配置（素数p∈{53,71,97,113,131}，三种随机种子）上验证FSD比Grokking平均提前1722步；(3) 通过分叉训练（在FSD达到峰值时改变权重衰减λ）提供因果证据，发现grokking时间与1/λ呈严格单调关系；(4) 进行架构消融实验（仅注意力、仅MLP、单层模型）。

Result: (1) FSD在所有9种配置中均比Grokking提前500-3000步（平均+1722步，符号检验p~0.004），且在所有9种情况中均优于Nanda等人的受限逻辑损失基线；(2) 在FSD峰值处分叉训练并改变λ，grokking时间Δ_t与1/λ呈严格线性关系（p=53和p=97时R²=1.00，p=131时R²=0.99）；(3) 仅注意力模型出现grokking并有强FSD前兆，仅MLP模型从不grok，单层模型的FSD滞后，证明FSD前兆是多块电路特性。

Conclusion: FSD是目前已知最早的Grokking预测指标；相位间差距本质上是正则化现象，权重衰减直接控制电路记忆权重的范数比，从而决定grokking时间；该机制具有跨架构和跨配置的可泛化性。

Abstract: Grokking -- where a transformer on modular arithmetic suddenly transitions from near-chance to near-perfect validation accuracy -- is attributed to a Fourier circuit, but its timing, causal structure, and controllability remain poorly understood. We introduce the Frequency Synchronization Degree (FSD), a normalised, permutation-tested metric for Fourier circuit synchronisation requiring no prior circuit knowledge. Across nine modular addition configurations (primes p in {53, 71, 97, 113, 131}, three seeds), FSD synchronises 500-3,000 steps before grokking (mean lead +1,722 steps; all nine positive, sign-test p~0.004), and precedes a restricted-logit loss baseline (Nanda et al.'s excluded loss) in all nine cases, making it the earliest available predictor. We provide direct causal evidence that the inter-phase gap is a regularisation phenomenon: forking training at the FSD-ceiling step and varying weight decay lambda produces strictly monotone earlier grokking, with Delta_t proportional to 1/lambda. This law replicates across three primes (p in {53,97,131}; R^2=1.00 and R^2=0.99 for two clean cases), captured as Delta_t ~ C/lambda, consistent with (1/lambda)*log(||W_mem||/tau). Architecture ablations show an attention-only model groks with a strong FSD precursor; an MLP-only model never groks; a single-layer model's FSD lags, confirming the precursor is a multi-block circuit property.

</details>


### [215] [Predicting Cognitive Load from Speech and Interaction Dynamics in Dyadic Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12971)
*Tahiya Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在自然协作对话中，语音和交互动态能否预测感知认知负荷。分析了53对双人进行9项协作任务的音频，提取声学、动态和交互特征，使用双头GRU编码器预测认知负荷。结果表明，对话交互信号能有效预测时间压力、脑力工作、努力和任务表现相关的认知负荷，其中时间需求与话轮转换动态相关，脑力需求与参与不平衡相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前认知负荷估计研究多局限于受控实验室环境，缺乏在自然协作对话中的可靠性验证。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究自然协作对话中语音和交互动态能否可靠预测感知认知负荷。

Method: 收集53对双人（共106人）进行9项协作任务的音频数据，提取静态声学特征、动态特征和交互特征，采用双头门控循环单元（GRU）编码器来预测认知负荷评分。

Result: 对话交互信号能够有效预测与时间压力、脑力工作、努力和任务表现相关的认知负荷。具体而言，时间需求与话轮重叠、说话者切换等话轮转换动态相关；脑力需求与双人之间参与不平衡相关。

Conclusion: 任务结构和对话交互对于在自然协作环境中建模认知负荷具有重要意义，未来应重视这些因素在设计认知负荷估计模型时的作用。

Abstract: Estimating cognitive load from speech has largely been studied in controlled laboratory settings, with limited understanding of its reliability in natural collaborative conversations. We investigate whether speech and interaction dynamics predict perceived cognitive load during dyadic conversations. We analyze audio from 53 dyads performing nine collaborative tasks and extract static acoustic, dynamic, and interaction features to train a two-head Gated Recurrent Unit encoder to predict cognitive load scores. Results show conversational interaction provides useful signals for predicting cognitive load related to time pressure, mental work, effort, and task performance. Temporal demand is associated with turn-taking dynamics such as overlap and speaker switch, while mental demand is linked to imbalanced participation between speakers. These findings highlight the importance of task structure and conversational interaction for modeling cognitive load in natural collaborative settings.

</details>


### [216] [EPM-JEPA: Operator-Side Experience Modulation in JEPA-Family World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12979)
*Vedant Pandya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种将累积经验注入JEPA预测器的方法（EI-JEPA和EPM-JEPA），发现EPM-JEPA（通过LoRA进行权重级调制）在分布偏移下表现优于无记忆基线，而EI-JEPA（隐藏状态残差注入）不如基线。主要贡献在于机制分析：揭示了D_shift轨迹由三种独立动力学过程驱动而非收敛到平衡态，并由此提出了PEM-JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA系列世界模型使用静态预测器，其权重在测试时动力学与训练分布偏离时无法自适应调整，因此需要探索将累积经验注入预测器的机制以应对分布偏移。

Method: 比较两种机制：操作数侧注入（EI-JEPA，将压缩经验表示作为残差添加到预测器隐藏状态）和算子侧调制（EPM-JEPA，通过LoRA生成低秩权重增量）。在Moving MNIST（重力偏移）上进行预先注册的比较实验。

Result: EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50}=0.7848±0.0078) 与 EI-JEPA (0.8238) 差异为4.74%（零结果）；EPM-JEPA比无记忆基线提升1.90%，EI-JEPA低于基线；D_shift轨迹反映三种独立动力学过程（缓冲循环、EMA目标漂移、LoRA固有暂态过程+0.021），而非收敛到平衡态。

Conclusion: 权重级调制（EPM-JEPA）优于隐藏状态注入（EI-JEPA），但主要贡献是揭示了当前方法存在动力学峰值而非收敛到平衡态的局限性，并由此提出了物理学启发的后继方法PEM-JEPA。

Abstract: JEPA-family world models use a static predictor whose weights do not adapt when test-time dynamics diverge from training. We compare two mechanisms for incorporating accumulated experience into a JEPA predictor under distribution shift: operand-side injection, where a compressed experience representation is added as a residual to the predictor's hidden state (EI-JEPA), and operator-side modulation, where the same representation generates low-rank weight deltas via LoRA applied to the predictor's weights (EPM-JEPA). On a pre-registered comparison (Moving MNIST, gravity shift), EPM-JEPA (D_shift^{n=50} = 0.7848 +/- 0.0078, three seeds) differs from EI-JEPA (0.8238) by delta = 4.74% - Outcome C: a null result - by our stated criterion, a valid outcome. As a secondary, non-pre-registered observation, EPM-JEPA improves 1.90% over a no-memory baseline (0.8000), consistently across seeds, while EI-JEPA underperforms the baseline, indicating the benefit is specific to weight-level modulation. Our primary contribution is a mechanism analysis: the D_shift^{n=50} trajectory reflects three independent dynamical processes - buffer cycling, EMA target drift, and an intrinsic LoRA settling transient of +0.021 - rather than convergence to equilibrium. These findings motivate PEM-JEPA, a physics-grounded successor addressing this dynamical-peak limitation.

</details>


### [217] [Exposure Bias as Epistemic Underidentification in Recursive Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12990)
*Riku Green,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Telmo M Silva Filho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明递归多步预测不仅是分布偏移问题，更是在部分可观测或状态截断条件下的认知不确认性问题。通过引入诱导状态Z和溯源变量P，分解了诱导状态误差的三个来源，并实验验证了闭环增益不仅来自局部适应，还来自改变滚动预测中访问的诱导状态。基于简单二元溯源的校正可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将递归多步预测视为分布偏移问题（训练时使用真实历史，部署时使用自身预测），但这种视角不完整。本文旨在揭示在部分可观测性或状态截断条件下，递归展开还存在认知不确认性（epistemic underidentification）问题，即使确定性潜在动力学下，一步贝叶斯监督也无法保证识别出部署时的递归预测器。

Method: 1) 形式化定义诱导状态Z和溯源变量P，推导诱导状态误差分解为三个项：教师强制/滚动预测不匹配、表示-类别近似误差、溯源信息缺口。2) 实验验证滚动预测进入不同的诱导状态空间，固定诱导状态定义独立的局部校正任务，闭环增益来自局部适应和诱导状态空间变化两方面。3) 提出基于简单二元溯源的溯源感知校正方法。

Result: 1) 滚动预测确实进入与训练时不同的诱导状态空间。2) 固定诱导状态可定义独立的局部校正任务。3) 闭环增益不仅来自局部适应，也来自滚动预测过程中访问的诱导状态集合的变化。4) 溯源感知校正可进一步提升性能，但增益是有条件的而非均匀的。

Conclusion: 应将暴露偏差（exposure bias）重新理解为在自诱导认知不确定性下的推理问题，而非单纯的分布偏移。这为理解和缓解递归多步预测中的泛化问题提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Recursive multi-step forecasting is usually framed as distribution shift: models are trained on observed histories but deployed on their own predictions. We show this framing is incomplete by proving that, under partial observability or state truncation, recursive rollout is also an epistemic underidentification problem. Even with deterministic latent dynamics, one-step Bayes supervision identifies behavior only on observed contexts and need not identify the deployed recursive predictor once rollout queries self-generated induced states whose correct local targets are not determined by numeric state alone. We formalize this with induced states $Z$ and provenance variables $P$, and derive a decomposition of induced-state error into teacher-forcing/rollout mismatch, representation--class approximation, and provenance information gaps. Empirically, we show that rollout enters a distinct induced-state regime, that fixed induced states define a distinct local corrective task, and that closed-loop gains arise not only from local adaptation but also from changing the induced states visited during rollout.
